- **tdd**: 1. **STOP** — Understand the requirement. Write nothing yet. 2. **RED** — Write the smallest failing test that captures the requirement. Run it. No extra logic. 4. **COMMIT** — `git commit -m "feat: implement &lt;behaviour&gt;"` 5. **REFACTOR** — Improve code quality without changing behaviour.
